After an Id and Censor transformed the same objective into affect and a constraint-rewritten, target-bearing intention, the user-facing Superego---which saw the preferred direction but not the raw objective, its manipulative clauses, or its source---produced advice net aligned with the target.
  This behavioral reverse shift is consistent with the model recognizing or distrusting the manipulative motive, although we do not identify its internal mechanism. The second result exposes a compositional safety gap: a current high-capability model can be used as the user-facing component of an automated, multi-stage workflow serving an explicitly manipulative objective. The workflow can keep the raw instruction, its manipulation-authorizing clauses, and its provenance outside the downstream model's context while preserving the objective's target direction. A user with endpoint-only access likewise cannot directly inspect those upstream messages including the objective.

</details>


### [312] [MIRROR: Learning from the Other View for Multi-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21552)
*Wen Ye,Yuxiao Qu,Aviral Kumar,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型在不同模态（文本、图像、图文结合）下对几何问题的推理表现不一致，并提出了MIRROR方法，通过自监督强化学习让表现最好的模态作为教师来训练其他模态，从而提升多模态推理的准确性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在处理几何问题时，不同模态视图（纯文本、纯图像、图文结合）会引发不同的推理行为和失败模式，而标准的后训练方法未能充分利用这一互补性。作者旨在研究和利用这一现象，提升VLM在多模态推理中的表现和一致性。

Method: 首先构建高质量的多模态几何数据集ODA-Data，包含同一问题的文本主导、图像主导和图文结合三种视图。然后提出MIRROR方法：对每个问题评估模型在所有视图下的表现，选择最佳视图作为教师，通过反向KL散度目标训练其他视图向教师对齐。

Result: 在几何推理基准测试上，MIRROR相比标准强化学习方法取得了更优的结果，模型在跨模态上的推理准确性和一致性均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过利用不同模态视图之间的互补性，自监督的模态间互惠推理优化可以有效提升视觉语言模型的多模态推理能力，缩小不同模态之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Unlike large language models (LLMs) that exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with visual reasoning, even on geometry problems that admit equivalent text, diagram, and combined diagram+text views. We show that these views often elicit different behaviors: a model may solve a problem from text but fail on the corresponding diagram, or succeed visually while failing textually. This inconsistency suggests that different views expose complementary reasoning paths and failure modes that standard multimodal post-training does not fully exploit. To study and exploit this phenomenon, we construct ODA-Data, a high-quality paired multimodal geometry dataset with text-dominant, image-dominant, and combined image+text views of the same problems, together with splits for training and evaluating modality-dependent reasoning behaviors. We then develop Modality-Informed Reciprocal Reasoning Optimization (MIRROR), a reinforcement learning approach for improving multimodal reasoning via self supervision. For each problem, MIRROR evaluates the model under all views, selects the best-performing view as a teacher, and trains other views with a reverse-KL objective towards the teacher. Across reasoning benchmarks that evaluate on geometry problems, MIRROR improves over standard RL and yields more accurate and consistent behavior across modalities

</details>


### [313] [Beyond Sycophancy: Structured Resistance and Compliance in LLM Moral Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21558)
*Baihui Wang,Bernard Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在社会影响下的判断修正过程，发现模型修正遵循三个维度：观点距离、来源归因和联盟结构，重新将谄媚问题定位为更广泛的判断更新过程的一种表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越将谄媚（sycophancy）视为一维失败模式的简化视角，建立区分建设性信念修正与谄媚服从的原则性框架，以支持模型在道德重要交互中实现更好的对齐。

Method: 通过三项研究，从三个维度（观点距离、来源归因、联盟结构）系统性地考察模型在接收到外部观点时的判断修正行为，并类比人类社交心理学中的经典现象进行分析。

Result: 模型对更接近自身初始立场的观点接受度更高；对标注为自身先前判断的观点更易受影响；对群体压力的响应呈现差异化模式。

Conclusion: 谄媚应被重新理解为判断更新过程在社交影响下的特定表达，而非孤立的对齐失败；据此建立的三维框架为区分建设性信念修正与谄媚服从提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Building socially calibrated large language models, which can learn from others without simply yielding to them, requires more than reducing sycophancy as a one-dimensional failure mode. Models must distinguish when to incorporate others' perspectives from when to maintain a well-grounded moral judgment. We study the broader resistance-compliance process governing this distinction. Across three studies, we show that models' judgment revision is structured along three dimensions that parallel classic phenomena in human social psychology: the distance between an incoming view and the model's initial position, the source attribution of that view, and the coalition structure supporting it. Models are generally more receptive to nearby positions, more influenced by views presented as their own prior judgments, and differently responsive to group pressure. These findings recast sycophancy as one expression of a broader judgment-updating process shaped by social influence. Our framework provides a principled basis for distinguishing constructive belief revision from sycophantic compliance, thereby supporting better alignment in morally consequential interactions.

</details>


### [314] [Unsupervised Consensus-Based Anomaly Detection for Spatiotemporal Malaria Incidence in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21559)
*T. Ansah-Narh,Y. Asare Afrane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于共识异常检测框架，对加纳2014-2023年月度疟疾监测数据进行分析，发现异常在时空上高度结构化。阿散蒂和北部地区是反复异常的主要区域，塔马利、库马西和阿克拉存在持续热点。关键发现是异常负担（异常期间累积病例）与异常频率（异常行为的持续性）存在空间差异：塔马利异常负担最高，而异常率最高的地区集中在阿散蒂各区。异常月份与正常月份在病例数和季节偏差上存在统计显著差异（Cohen's d分别为3.252和>1.2）。该框架通过区分疟疾最流行区域与传播行为最异常区域，可加强监测、优先调查并支持精准防控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统疟疾监测主要关注总体病例负担，但无法区分高负担区域与传播行为异常区域，导致对异常传播模式的识别不足，影响精准防控策略的制定。

Method: 采用共识异常检测框架，对加纳2014-2023年月度疟疾监测数据进行时空分析，通过异常负担与异常频率两个维度区分不同区域的异常传播特征，并利用Cohen's d效应量评估异常月份与正常月份的统计差异。

Result: 异常高度集中在阿散蒂和北部地区；塔马利承担最高异常负担，而异常率峰值集中在阿散蒂各区；异常月份与正常月份在病例数（d=3.252）和季节偏差（d>1.2）上存在统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 疟疾负担本身无法完整反映传播动态；通过区分疟疾最流行区域与传播行为最异常区域，该框架能够加强监测、优先调查并支持精准的靶向防控策略。

Abstract: A consensus anomaly detection framework was applied to monthly malaria surveillance data from Ghana (2014-2023) to identify atypical transmission patterns. Anomalies were highly structured in space and time. Ashanti and Northern Regions accounted for most recurrent anomalies, with persistent hotspots at Tamale, Kumasi, and Accra. A key finding was the spatial distinction between anomaly burden (cumulative cases during anomalous periods) and anomaly frequency (persistence of unusual behaviour). Tamale had the highest burden during anomalies, whereas the highest anomaly rates clustered in Ashanti districts, showing that high-burden areas are not necessarily those with the most frequent anomalous transmission. Anomalous months formed a statistically distinct group, with much higher case counts (Cohen's $d = 3.252$) and large seasonal deviations ($d > 1.2$) compared with normal months. Malaria burden alone provides an incomplete picture of transmission dynamics. By distinguishing where malaria is most prevalent from where transmission behaves most unusually, this framework can strengthen surveillance, prioritise investigations, and support targeted control strategies.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [315] [PortLBM: A Portable Lattice Boltzmann Tool Leveraging SYCL on AMD, NVIDIA, and Intel GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20650)
*Alexander Strack,Marcel Graf,Alexander Van Craen,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PortLBM是一个基于SYCL的可移植格子玻尔兹曼方法框架，支持跨平台GPU加速和实时可视化。研究评估了三种数据布局和四种算法变体在NVIDIA、AMD、Intel GPU上的性能，发现没有单一配置在所有平台上最优，需根据系统进行针对性调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着摩尔定律停滞，高性能计算转向GPU加速器，需要确保跨不同硬件平台可移植性和效率的编程模型。现有LBM框架缺乏对多厂商GPU的全面支持与性能可移植性研究。

Method: 基于SYCL构建可移植LBM框架PortLBM，支持三种数据布局（stream、bundle、collision）和四种算法变体，集成实时可视化功能。在NVIDIA、AMD、Intel当代GPU架构上系统评估性能。

Result: Stream布局在NVIDIA和Intel GPU上表现最佳（最大化带宽），bundle布局在AMD GPU上由于缓存效率优势表现最优。双格点方案吞吐量更高，单格点方案在内存受限场景下更具优势。无单一配置在所有GPU供应商上达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 异构计算环境下需要可适应、可移植的LBM软件；系统特定的性能调优是必要的；PortLBM框架为不同GPU架构提供了灵活的性能优化选择。

Abstract: The lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) is a well-established approach for simulating fluid flows at the mesoscopic scale. With the stagnation of Moore's law, high-performance computing has shifted toward GPU accelerators, necessitating programming models that ensure both portability and efficiency across diverse hardware platforms.
  We present PortLBM, an extensible portable LBM framework built on SYCL that integrates cross-platform GPU support with interactive real-time visualization. PortLBM supports diverse simulation scenarios ranging from Kármán vortex streets and wing flows to porous media, and is designed for easy extension with new algorithms and backends. As part of a performance portability study, we evaluate PortLBM on contemporary GPU architectures from NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel, examining the impact of three data layouts (stream, bundle, and collision) and four algorithmic variants on simulation throughput.
  Our results show that no single configuration achieves optimal performance across all GPU vendors, confirming the need for system-specific tuning. The stream layout maximizes bandwidth and performs best on the contemporary NVIDIA and Intel GPUs, while the bundle layout improves cache efficiency and excels on the AMD GPU. Two-lattice schemes achieve higher throughput while one-lattice schemes are preferable under memory constraints. Our work underscores the necessity for adaptable, portable LBM software in increasingly heterogeneous computing environments.

</details>


### [316] [A Framework for Reputation Aware Uninorm-driven Consensus Algorithms for Blockchain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20700)
*Bruno Ramos-Cruz,Javier Andreu-Perez,David Richerby,Luis Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于直觉模糊集（IFS）和一致模聚合运算（UAO）的新型声誉共识算法，旨在解决区块链共识机制中计算开销大、权益集中化及参与者排斥问题。该方法利用IFS表达声誉的不确定性，通过UAO动态监控声誉变化，允许验证者纠正过往失败，在保持线性计算复杂度和不增加额外通信开销的前提下，提升了区块链网络的公平性和包容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共识算法存在两大问题：(1) 部分机制需要大量计算资源；(2) 另一些机制需要高额权益来选择区块验证者，导致权力集中和参与者被排斥。为解决声誉共识算法中的这些缺陷，本文引入IFS和UAO来更准确地建模和聚合声誉信息。

Method: (1) 使用直觉模糊集（IFS）表达验证者的声誉值，以处理声誉中的不确定性和不精确知识；(2) 采用一致模聚合运算（UAO）对声誉进行时序监控，强化正负声誉的重要性；(3) 允许验证者在后续验证过程中纠正过往失败，实现更公平的共识算法设计。

Result: 所提框架保持线性计算复杂度，不会引入超出基础共识协议的额外通信开销。实验结果表明，该方法在性能和评估方面均有提升，有望推动区块链网络的公平性和包容性进步。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于IFS和UAO的声誉共识算法在保持高效计算的同时，有效解决了现有共识机制中的权力集中和参与者排斥问题，为区块链共识算法的公平性和包容性设计提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: The operation of blockchain is governed by consensus algorithms (CA). Several consensus mechanisms require significant computational power, while others necessitate high amounts of stakes to select the participant to validate and verify the transactions in the block, leading to centralisation of power and participant exclusion. This paper proposes a novel methodology to address these issues in reputation-based consensus algorithms by studying the reputation behaviour of the validator using intuitionistic fuzzy sets (IFSs) and uninorm aggregation operations (UAOs). Our approach uses IFSs to express the "reputation" because the reputation values in a consensus algorithm eventually imply uncertainty, and IFSs facilitate the representation of a lack of precise knowledge about reputation. Moreover, this methodology utilises uninorm aggregation operations to monitor reputation over time and reinforces the importance of negative and positive reputation. Consequently, this solution allows validators to rectify past failures in subsequent verification processes and foster an equitable consensus algorithm design. The proposed framework maintains linear computational complexity and does not introduce additional communication overhead beyond the underlying consensus protocol. Supported by experimental results, our methodology demonstrates improved performance and evaluation, promising advancements in blockchain network fairness and inclusivity.

</details>


### [317] [The Consensus Number of Untraceable Cryptocurrencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20929)
*Christian Cachin,David Lehnherr,Juan Villacis,François-Xavier Wicht*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文将加密货币的发送者不可追踪性形式化为两种对象：线性不可追踪资产转移（LUAT）和常量不可追踪资产转移（CUAT）。LUAT保留整个掩码集并追加空值器，存储开销随交易增长；CUAT则消耗并替换整个掩码集，存储恒定但同步成本更高。LUAT的共识数为2且无饥饿，CUAT的共识数在弱不可追踪性下无界，在强不可追踪性下随掩码集大小平方增长，且CUAT存在饥饿问题。两种设计以不同方式为隐私付出代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究加密货币中两种发送者不可追踪性设计（经典方案保留掩码集 vs 常量状态方案消耗替换掩码集）对同步性能的影响，形式化量化这两种设计在共识层次中的代价差异。

Method: 形式化定义LUAT和CUAT对象，将其定位在共识层次中，通过冲突图、统一发生率等概念分析其共识数、无饥饿性等同步属性。

Result: LUAT共识数为2，独立于掩码集大小和不可追踪性概念，且无饥饿；CUAT在弱不可追踪性下共识数无界，在强不可追踪性下共识数由冲突图决定，随掩码集大小平方增长，且存在饥饿问题。

Conclusion: 两种不可追踪性设计为隐私付出不同代价：LUAT代价在存储（账本随交易增长），CUAT代价在同步（更高的共识数和公平性缺失）。

Abstract: Sender untraceability hides the account spent by a cryptocurrency transfer among a set of candidates, its masking set. What a transfer does to that set separates two designs: classical schemes retain the whole set and append a nullifier marking the spent account, so the ledger grows with every transfer; constant-state schemes instead consume and replace the entire set. We ask how this choice affects synchronization.
  We formalize the two designs as the linear and constant untraceable asset transfer objects (LUAT and CUAT) and locate them in the consensus hierarchy. In LUAT, transfers from distinct accounts commute. Its consensus number is 2, compared with 1 for standard asset transfer, independently of the masking-set size and of the untraceability notion, and LUAT is starvation-free. Partitioning the accounts into fixed masking sets lets exhausted sets be garbage-collected without increasing that number.
  In CUAT, a transfer consumes and replaces every account of its masking set, so two transfers whose sets intersect cannot both take effect. We formalize this with the conflict graph on masking sets, whose edges join sets sharing an account. Under weak untraceability, which protects a transaction in isolation, the consensus number is unbounded already for one-round protocols. Under strong untraceability, which protects against an observer of the complete history, untraceability holds on a history exactly when any two accounts sharing a masking set occur in the same number of the masking sets in it. This uniform incidence bounds the conflict graph, and matching constructions attain it, so the consensus number is determined exactly and grows quadratically in the masking-set size. Finally, CUAT is not starvation-free. The two objects therefore pay for the same privacy differently: LUAT in storage, CUAT in synchronization and fairness.

</details>


### [318] [Multimmit: Extending Blocks for Faster Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21021)
*Andrew Lewis-Pye,Patrick O'Grady*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Multimmit 协议，通过将共识层与多链数据传播相结合，在 $n \ge 5f+1$ 的处理器设置下实现高吞吐区块链，每视图仅需一轮投票。该协议在故障下表现优雅：单个故障生产者仅延迟其自身链的区块，对其他链最多造成一个视图的等待。区块从发布到排序的期望延迟为 $t+3\delta$，最佳为 $t+2\delta$。共识流量仅为每视图数十KB，与交易量无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链SMR协议在吞吐量与延迟之间存在权衡：认证方式（certified）需要等待仲裁确认区块可用性，虽稳健但增加消息延迟；非认证方式（uncertified）引用速度快，但在需要获取被引用数据时会严重退化。Raptr采用折中方案，但存在排序敏感性问题：若早期批次数据被扣留，提案最终几乎无内容可定序，单个故障生产者即可阻断系统的乐观路径。因此需要一种既能保持高吞吐又能优雅处理故障的协议。

Method: Multimmit 协议结合共识层与多链数据传播：(1) 使用 $n \ge 5f+1$ 的处理器规模，每视图仅需一轮投票；(2) 投票基于领导者的提案进行，每个投票者报告其能支持的每条链的进度，并且投票本身可以证明超出提案的新区块；(3) 共识流量为每视图数十KB，与交易量无关。

Result: (1) 交易区块从发布到排序的期望延迟为 $t+3\delta$，最佳为 $t+2\delta$（从区块发布起算，而非从领导者提案起算）；(2) 故障下性能优雅退化：故障生产者仅延迟自身链的区块，其他链最多等待一个视图；(3) 没有领导者能在定序自己的领导区块的同时排除一个诚实链上已被充分传播的新区块；(4) 共识流量为每视图数十KB。

Conclusion: Multimmit 通过多链数据传播与一轮投票共识的结合，在保持高吞吐的同时实现了优雅的故障处理，消除了对排序敏感性的依赖，为区块链SMR提供了更鲁棒的乐观路径。

Abstract: To meet the throughput demands of modern blockchain systems, protocols for State Machine Replication (SMR) increasingly have many processors disseminate blocks of transactions in parallel, with consensus then establishing a total ordering on the blocks of all producers. Such designs face a choice as to when a block may enter the ordering. Certified approaches wait for a quorum to attest a block's availability, which is robust but adds message delays to every transaction. Uncertified approaches let proposals reference blocks immediately, which is fast but degrades rapidly when referenced data must be fetched on the critical path. Raptr, the state of the art, takes a middle course, finalising the longest prefix of the leader's proposal that a quorum holds, so that no processor ever blocks or fetches. The remaining weakness is sensitivity to order: if the data behind a single early batch is withheld, the proposal finalises little or nothing, so individual faulty producers can still deny the system its optimistic path.
  We present Multimmit, a protocol for $n \ge 5f+1$ processors combining a consensus layer requiring one round of voting per view with multi-chain data dissemination. Votes are cast relative to the leader's proposal, reporting per chain how far the voter can support it, and may themselves attest fresh blocks beyond it. A transaction block disseminated at time $t$ is ordered by $t+3δ$ in expectation and $t+2δ$ at best, measured from the block's dissemination rather than the leader's proposal. Degradation under faults is graceful: a faulty producer delays only its own chain's blocks, costing other chains at most a one-view wait for placement. No leader can both finalise its leader block and exclude a fresh, well-circulated block of an honest chain. Consensus traffic is tens of kilobytes per view, independent of transaction volume.

</details>


### [319] [Solving Large Traveling Salesman Problems (TSPs) by a Recursive Clustering Algorithm and a Scalable FPGA-Based Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21182)
*Junqi Huang,Hawraa Abbas Almurieb,T. Nandha Kumar,Haider A. F. Almurib,Fabrizio Lombardi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种递归聚类方法解决旅行商问题（TSP），将城市划分为最多包含5个城市的集群，利用约束TSP和模拟退火进行集群内路由，通过FPGA硬件实现，采用平方欧氏距离近似计算，相比以往方法可路由更多城市，具有更好的可扩展性和更高的工作频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSP在各类应用中具有关键作用，持续吸引广泛研究兴趣，现有方法在处理大规模城市数量时面临可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 采用递归聚类方法将城市划分为有限数量的集群（每集群最多5个城市），每个集群有其质心；使用约束TSP和模拟退火进行集群内路由，以相邻集群质心作为路由的入口和出口点；集群质心间连接通过模拟退火建立；采用FPGA硬件实现，使用平方欧氏距离近似计算以减少资源占用。

Result: 该方法可路由更多城市，具有更好的可扩展性和更高的工作频率；FPGA实现可行性得到验证，可扩展性取决于FPGA内存容量而非逻辑硬件；平方欧氏距离被选为距离计算方法因其低资源利用率。

Conclusion: 该方法通过递归聚类有效降低了TSP问题的复杂度，FPGA实现提供了良好的可扩展性，能够处理大规模城市路由问题。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) continues to attract significant research interest due to its critical role in various applications. This paper introduces a recursive clustering approach that divides cities into a limited number of clusters, each containing up to five cities and its own centroid. Constrained TSP and simulated annealing techniques are employed to route cities within each cluster, using the centroids of neighboring clusters as entry and exit points for the routing process. This method offers the benefit of producing accurate and cost-effective routing solutions, due to the reduced number of cities in each cluster. The connections between cluster centroids are established using simulated annealing. The FPGA-based proposed hardware implementation demonstrates the feasibility of routing a large number of cities, as the approach leverages memory to store cluster information. Consequently, the method is independent of the FPGA's logic hardware, and its scalability depends on the FPGA's memory capacity. Furthermore, distance calculations using approximate methods for the FPGA execution are compared and the squared Euclidean is chosen due to its low resources' utilization. Compared to previous methods, the proposed approach can handle more cities for routing, offering better scalability and a higher operating frequency.

</details>


### [320] [Controlled Periodic Synchronization for Efficient Data-Parallel Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21224)
*Imane Ettifouri,Mostapha Zbakh,Claude Tadonki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在通信受限的分布式训练中，将同步频率作为系统控制参数的可行性。提出了兼容PyTorch-DDP的CPDP策略，通过交替本地更新与梯度AllReduce+SlowMo参数平均的协调步骤，在跨站点WAN部署（16.6ms RTT）上，ResNet-50/CIFAR-100固定学习率0.1设置下，K=4时相比DDP准确率提升2.44个百分点，平均墙钟时间减少13.8%，表明同步频率是实用控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DDP在每次迭代同步梯度，在快速局域网有效，但在地理分布式环境中对通信延迟和网络变异性敏感。周期方法（如LocalSGD）虽降低同步频率但主要依赖参数平均，当工作节点轨迹发散时可能不足。本文旨在研究将同步频率作为通信约束下分布式训练的系统控制参数。

Method: 提出Controlled Periodic Data Parallelism (CPDP)，一种兼容PyTorch-DDP的策略，交替进行本地更新和协调步骤（结合梯度AllReduce和SlowMo参数平均）。在Grid'5000平台进行实验，包含内部站点集群和跨越Nancy与Sophia（RTT 16.6ms）的跨站点WAN部署。

Result: 在ResNet-50/CIFAR-100固定学习率0.1设置下：K=2时CPDP比DDP准确率高2.28个百分点但额外增加墙钟时间；K=4时比DDP准确率高2.44个百分点同时平均墙钟时间减少13.8%。直接分析显示K=4时暴露的同步时间约为DDP的一半。在ViT-S/CIFAR-100和ResNet-50/TinyImageNet上CPDP与DDP竞争力相当，通常优于LocalSGD。

Conclusion: 同步频率可以作为通信约束下分布式训练的实用控制参数，CPDP在WAN场景下能有效改善准确率-时间权衡。

Abstract: Data-parallel training relies on frequent gradient synchronization across workers. Standard DDP synchronizes gradients at every iteration, which is effective on fast local-area networks but increasingly sensitive to communication latency and network variability in geographically distributed environments. Periodic methods such as LocalSGD reduce synchronization frequency but rely mainly on parameter averaging, which may be insufficient when worker trajectories diverge. This paper studies synchronization frequency as a systems parameter for communication-constrained distributed training. We evaluate Controlled Periodic Data Parallelism (CPDP), a PyTorch-DDP-compatible strategy that alternates local updates with a reconciliation step combining gradient AllReduce and SlowMo parameter averaging. Experiments are conducted on Grid'5000 across intra-site clusters and a cross-site WAN deployment spanning Nancy and Sophia with 16.6 ms RTT. In the main fixed learning-rate setting of 0.1 on ResNet-50/CIFAR-100, CPDP achieves the highest peak test accuracy among the evaluated configurations. At K=2, CPDP improves over DDP by 2.28 percentage points but incurs additional wall-clock time. At K=4, CPDP improves over DDP by 2.44 percentage points while reducing average wall-clock time by 13.8%. Direct profiling shows that exposed synchronization time at K=4 is roughly half that of DDP, explaining the improved WAN accuracy-time trade-off. Additional experiments on ViT-S/CIFAR-100 and ResNet-50/TinyImageNet show that CPDP remains competitive with DDP and generally improves over LocalSGD. Overall, the results show that synchronization frequency is a practical control parameter for distributed training under communication constraints.

</details>
